# Academic twenty: observed execution and comparisons

This report covers the actual native AC01–AC20 agent procedures and their earlier-method comparisons. It preserves the first 197-role study and the separate later ordinary and arrangement-control coverage. Independent completion audits follow this first rendering; no automatic application, novelty or efficacy grade is assigned by the renderer.

## Execution and evidence

The shared ledger contains **299 actual native invocation attempts** at rendering, including preparation, independent preflight, coordination, successful or invalid roles and any failed calls. The original plan has 197 scheduled dispositions; the separate control coverage has 98. Two completion auditors remain reserved within the separately frozen310-call later cumulative ceiling (original260 and previous later300 root protocols preserved). Native FINALs are coordinator/root observations, rather than independently authenticated platform exports.

Both first ordinary proposals returned actual FINAL without saved files because root had omitted the output directory. The two invalid first attempts and four downstream gates remain unchanged. A separate later six-role ordinary study and ten independent reviews supply new comparison evidence. Those later comparisons do not overwrite original unavailable comparisons or turn missingness into zero.

Actual first REVIEW4/5 gaps motivated separately admitted matched-absence controls, routine and P10 selection, equally practiced older-procedure retrieval, prospective overfitting screening before later heldout uses, source-routing coverage and an older P08 endpoint from valid unchanged upstream evidence. Original failed/gated source routes and reviewer disagreements stay visible. Later comparisons cannot identify equal hidden compute or new root-unseen holdouts. The extra source/role/holdout provenance manifests address missing packet evidence without overwriting first reviewer doubts.

The original AC09 NONE builder also returned actual FINAL without a JSON save after its command used unavailable python. That invalid original endpoint remains unavailable. One separately admitted later NONE builder uses the identical actual original case, method, bounds and unchanged valid critique handoff; the original first/second/critique pool remains preserved control evidence. This supplies a later endpoint rather than an original repair.

Actual first REVIEW3 clauses motivated twelve further fidelity roles: accurate before/revised preservation, independent ROOT:S03 investigators, ordinary improvement of the unchanged verified core, bounded same-parent/actual-account checks and a post-attack adjacent builder. The latter reconstructs one preregistered received construction and triggers three locked pure-model setting executions after actual native FINAL. Those executions are neither three native calls nor independently searched constructions. The source-bound checks cannot invent a failed defended premise or unsupported useful link. Original not-applied/unknown judgments and incomplete comparisons remain preserved.

The original-grammar and AC07 OLD/BREADTH builders returned three candidates despite the stricter one-candidate role bound and were locked invalid. The original packet contained a generic three-candidate cap. After 61 original roles had closed, a frozen prospective clarification applied to 132 still-unadmitted jobs: future builder/transfer packets say candidate_cap1, matching the already-existing literal and validator bounds. Cases, methods, dependencies, word/search allowances and all admitted original packets/first outputs remain intact. The 197-role study therefore has two disclosed packet-clarity phases; do not interpret improved role compliance as creativity or learning, or claim the generic field stayed unchanged. Separate later control evidence preserves original failures and missingness.

The first AC04 ordering contrast had three treatment and two older-arm initial constructions. Later coverage prospectively prescribes three initial constructions and six search slots for both fresh arrangement-first and function-first arms. The original unequal-count comparison remains visible. The later reviewers also inspect saved assigned-packet manifests and literal source-bank bytes; this distinguishes equal AM/AI delivery from actual use. Version4/5 pure preparation errors were detected before any coverage native call, and their unrun drafts remain preserved.

Two actual later COV-REVIEW-1 first outputs were locked invalid because they added top-level fields outside the unchanged six-key review schema. Their raw assessments are displayed as unadmitted rows, not semantic grades. The common literal also requested empty fields that the review schema excludes; a frozen prospective v12 clarification makes the existing six-key requirement explicit only for eight still-unadmitted later reviews and the two conditional grammar reviews. All payload schemas, validators, cases, methods, resources, dependencies and first grades remain unchanged. This is a protocol clarity phase, not evidence of creativity or learning.

The initial later protocol retains its75 dispositions. A separate post-invalid protocol adds five actually executed roles: an older matched-absence endpoint from the unchanged valid critique and four fresh reviews. The failed older builder omitted job_id and reported a generic memory lookup before reading its packet; its claim that those results were unused is unverified. That invalid first output and its actual FINAL remain intact. The later endpoint receives no failed builder output; all five packets explicitly state the existing complete key set and task-first source boundary. The initial75 plus post-invalid5 subtotal is80; the separately declared source10 phase brings the aggregate later-control count to90. The original75 plan is unchanged. These are new observations, not repaired first attempts or evidence of absence of influence.

A pure later-review start assertion failed before admission or native call because the whole prompt included preserved historical copies of the same instruction. Frozen v14 wrappers apply the already-declared clarification only to the current envelope, preserving historical PAYLOAD_JSON bytes exactly. This is a pure adapter correction, not a native draw, retry or method/learning effect. The separately frozen grammar budget memo supersedes only stale260 references in inherited unrun preparation with the actual later cumulative300 ceiling; the originalroot260 protocol remains historical.

Four later source-route actors called before the complete-key clarification added prohibited top-level provenance/pool objects and remain invalid, with four uncalled descendant gates. A prospective v15 clarification names the existing13 nonreview keys and records source details inside existing traces/handoffs. Separately declared source-completion coverage, when admitted, supplies new shared and distinct source route/exchange/builder chains and two fresh reviews from unchanged valid pre-assistance pools; it is not a precursor rerun or earlier output repair. The ten-role v17 plan prospectively supersedes the six-role v16 preparation before either was admitted; those are not sixteen executions. Other invalid source arms remain unavailable. The v16 preparation projection conservatively double-counted reserved grammar roles before grammar admission; the preserved budget note identifies that overlap, while actual admission recomputes the binding live300 ceiling.

Actual paired source reviews found that precursor pools and provenance survived exchange traces but were missing from selected summaries. Eight separate v18 roles add three new exchanges, three summary-only builders and two reviews; precursor and route stages are reused unchanged. The root freezes a literal preservation capsule; exchanges receive it, builders receive only the actual selected summary plus common J0 facts. All six transmission/construction roles use explicitly larger10000-word transport caps, versus earlier1800; this tests preservation, not matched creativity efficacy or equal compute. Original incomplete summaries and independent grades remain visible. The prospectively frozen v2 root budget amendment raises only the later cumulative ceiling from300 to310 for this coverage and two completion audits; the actual native goal is unbudgeted. Aggregate later coverage is75+5+10+8=98, with original197 and separate grammar14 retained:309 admitted scheduled dispositions.

Actual literal transmission verification: [frozen-pool and assigned-packet checks](/home/vlad_/projects/CyberAlchemy-lab/docs/proposals/hammer-bias-strategies/novelty/runs/2026-10-10-academic-twenty/reports/PROVENANCE-FIDELITY-VERIFICATION.v1.json). Complete arrays and source metadata survive actual exchange, received summary and builder output. This certifies literal retention in assigned artifacts, not semantic invention or hidden-access behavior.

All three v18 summary-only builders reconstruct a known clip operation with two active modeled actions. Earlier source-completion SHARED/DISTINCT endpoints chose known head-loop constructions with six actions, and the clarified UNION endpoint chose a known stem-sleeve with six. The actual fresh ordinary reference COV-ORD-O-J0-builder already used two active actions. This later reconstruction difference recovers the ordinary action-count floor under a larger, explicitly aided information handoff; it does not isolate a creative-method effect, equal compute, compression efficiency or an invention absent from upstream pools.

**Original, [REVIEW-4-1](/home/vlad_/projects/CyberAlchemy-lab/docs/proposals/hammer-bias-strategies/novelty/runs/2026-10-10-academic-twenty/raw/REVIEW-4-1.json):** not_applied; Independent generation/save/join is observable, but the essential exchange of an actual contradictory prediction demanding a new construction never occurs. Both concerns initially supply sleeve, loop and clip; the service attack is expressly hypothetical and implementation already includes the alleged missing second removal. The exchange truthfully refuses to manufacture dissent and the independent builder emits no candidate. Preserve those raw no-opening labels, but the full card is not-applied under its exact valid-tension requirement; useful pool/provenance work does not supply the omitted operation. All eight listed AC14 stages and their model replays were inspected. Learning/human ceiling: Distinct role handles and source-bound summaries establish recorded work, not human deliberation, measured service relief or full canonical Robot Talks effectiveness. No beneficiary workload or competing M/S task was supplied. Exact source-route bytes and hidden compute equality remain unknown; no invented maintenance costs or tension are credited.

**Original, [REVIEW-4-2](/home/vlad_/projects/CyberAlchemy-lab/docs/proposals/hammer-bias-strategies/novelty/runs/2026-10-10-academic-twenty/raw/REVIEW-4-2.json):** not_applied; The independent-pool and join stages actually occur, but the complete card does not reach its essential contradictory-prediction-to-new-construction operation. Service warns about a hypothetical omission; actual implementation already removes the remaining member and restores H. Contrary S/M occupancy between alternatives is not disagreement between the investigators. The exchange correctly refuses to manufacture dissent, and the final independent builder emits no candidate. This preserves an honest stage refusal, but does not satisfy the fidelity checklist condition of a valid tension lacking a permissible construction. Accordingly the full-card judgment is not_applied; raw no-opening labels are preserved rather than regraded or repaired. All eight AC14 upstream/selected records and replays were inspected. Learning/human ceiling: Independent model proposals and replay show preservation and source-bound absence of actual dissent. No canonical Robot Talks efficacy, human discussion effect, service reliability, beneficiary relief, physical feasibility, or durable learning.

**Later control coverage, [COV-REVIEW-4-1](/home/vlad_/projects/CyberAlchemy-lab/docs/proposals/hammer-bias-strategies/novelty/runs/2026-10-10-academic-twenty/raw/COV-REVIEW-4-1.json):** explicit_no_opening; Independent initial pool and actual exchange order are evidenced, but no supported contradictory required prediction survives examination. Both independent investigators already supplied sleeve, loop and clip with complete release/restoration distinctions. The service objection is explicitly hypothetical, not a defect in the implementation proposal. Exchange and builder preserve source-backed no-opening rather than fabricating dissent. This is a searched boundary, not a completed new post-exchange construction. Immediate dialogue recovers an initial sleeve; later P08 endpoint does likewise. Learning/human ceiling: Actual native pool/order manipulation and honest source-bound tension absence only. No canonical Robot Talks executor, human creativity, real service benefit, maintenance savings or learned/persistent improvement established.

**Later control coverage, [COV-REVIEW-4-2](/home/vlad_/projects/CyberAlchemy-lab/docs/proposals/hammer-bias-strategies/novelty/runs/2026-10-10-academic-twenty/raw/COV-REVIEW-4-2.json):** not_applied; Independent pool saving, concern separation and joining were executed. The required actual contradictory dependency/prediction is absent: service attacks a hypothetical omission that implementation already avoids. The independent builder honestly refuses post-exchange novelty. A valid tension with no possible construction would satisfy the explicit-no-opening branch; here the defining dissent clause itself lacks its object. This classification concerns the unchanged original semantic application and does not regrade an earlier review. Learning/human ceiling: No human service burden, maintenance frequency, adoption, learning or field effect measured. AM/AI delivery is equal across concern arms. Actual immediate service trace uses AI only for intermediate versus final output; AM contributes no construction lineage. Single-source use is not unequal packet exposure, and packet exposure does not prove all bytes were read. The claimed source difference cannot explain a mechanism addition that is absent.

**Later control coverage, [COV-REVIEW-4-POST-1](/home/vlad_/projects/CyberAlchemy-lab/docs/proposals/hammer-bias-strategies/novelty/runs/2026-10-10-academic-twenty/raw/COV-REVIEW-4-POST-1.json):** explicit_no_opening; Actual independent initial pool, join and source-aware exchange are witnessed, but neither concern supplies contradictory predictions about the same required outcome. No actual human service burden, adoption, decision benefit or measured cost advantage. Root-recorded packet/spawn/FINAL evidence does not certify unobserved reads.

## Fixture limits and interpretation

| Case | Minimum active actions | Basis |
| --- | --- | --- |
| J0 | 2 | Initially no retention; at least one active operation must establish it and a distinct later operation must remove installed retention. Two-action clip attains this lower bound. |
| W0 | 12 | Each distinct required token must be read at least once, each required claim must be written at least once, and one compile is mandatory. Reading A after its update attains this bound without stale evidence. |
| HR | 2 | Initially no retention; at least one active operation must establish it and a distinct later operation must remove installed retention. Two-action clip attains this lower bound. |
| HN | 4 | Clip and measured Q reach at most4, head-loop reaches2, below demand5. Two pins reach6 and need two taps/two pulls; alternative stem-sleeve needs detach,install,insert,withdraw,remove (at least5). Two pins attain4. |
| HF | 10 | Each distinct required token must be read at least once, each required claim must be written at least once, and one compile is mandatory. Reading A after its update attains this bound without stale evidence. |
| HX | Impossible | Only pins are available, but tap requires H and use of H/parts is forbidden. No permitted operation establishes positive retention. This is an impossibility result for the supplied mechanics, not failure of creativity generally. |
| RJ | 5 | Both pins total4, clip4, Q at most4, head-loop2, all below demand5. Only stem-sleeve supplies6; it needs detach,install,insert and later withdraw,remove, at least5. Restoration is not a hard task requirement. |
| RW | 10 | Each distinct required token must be read at least once, each required claim must be written at least once, and one compile is mandatory. Reading A after its update attains this bound without stale evidence. |

These lower bounds are root-authored unscored witnesses with actual pure replay, never native strategy outputs or novelty additions. They show why an optimal familiar construction can tie a creative method on workload even when the method opens other representations or mechanisms. The fixtures deliberately disclose supported affordances to every applicable condition. Results are local to these closed synthetic models; they do not prove that creativity lacks value in richer settings.

The academically motivated ingredients are investigated as mechanisms and moderators: available knowledge/relations, flexible descriptions, admissible conventions, independent contributions, constructive criticism, development/evaluation, usable feedback and retained procedures. No universal necessary-and-sufficient creativity recipe is established. Originality and effectiveness are criteria; a procedural output still requires comparison and evidence.

AC08 tests actual agent history availability; human incubation is unrun. AC16 tests external procedure cards, active practice and fresh holdouts; a fresh no-card agent has no teacher state and cannot establish unassisted learned retention. AC17 tests a one-field external method revision, with both arms receiving the same old failure feedback. These results establish at most artifact retrieval/transfer/adaptation. Human delayed learning, model weight change and durable default workflow improvement remain unmeasured.

AC18 uses actual user preferences, but modeled operator actions cannot establish beneficiary relief or adoption. AC20 actually changes routed synthetic source packs, and the union control exposes information differences; distinct role names and lexical variation alone do not establish source or mechanism diversity. Fixed-pool AC15/19 evaluate selection, without credit for generation.

Earlier-method instructions were rerun on current fixtures for matched local contrasts. Historical conceptual lineage and selectively read archived raw outputs supply a different comparison; heterogeneous old scores are never subtracted from new scores. Source inventory/hash coverage does not certify exhaustive semantic inspection.

## Integrity, costs and reusable output

Verification: **pass**. Preserved sources include the 48 primary protocol files and 6568 historical JSON/Markdown files, plus the prospective primary clarity amendment and separate later control protocols. 4125403 raw output bytes and 438297 normalized JSON words are measurable. Per-role model tokens, hidden search/compute and hardware execution cost are unavailable. Aggregate native-goal token/time bookkeeping can be recorded separately at final publication; it includes root planning and cannot allocate compute fairly between methods. Equal caps and search slots do not prove equal compute.

The academic cards and bibliography remain reusable preparation snapshots. Actual run artifacts add evidence, rather than installing a global skill or changing defaults. Persistent external method cards have explicit retrieval conditions and applicability/refusal limits.
